Across all pruning configurations, SNIPER achieves an excellent mean rank of 1.25, indicating its robust cross-architectural generalizability and excellent reliability.

</details>


### [144] [LycheeMemory V2: Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents via Semantic Segment-Level Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12990)
*Dongfang Li,Zixuan Liu,Junmai Wang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Bonian Jia,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LycheeMemory V2，通过语义分段替代逐轮整合，大幅降低长期记忆构建成本并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程LLM代理的记忆系统依赖“急切整合”（每次交互后调用LLM提取/总结），导致随对话增长构建成本急剧上升；粗粒度总结易丢失细节，而扩大检索上下文则增加查询开销。

Method: 引入语义分段级整合机制：将多次交互批量为语义片段，检测语义边界以保留连贯的事件与时间证据，将每个最终化片段编码为上下文独立的类型化记忆记录，并使用轻量级结构化索引支持查询规划的证据检索。

Result: 在GPT-4.1-Mini实验下，LoCoMo准确率达89.22%，LongMemEval-S达92.20%（SOTA）。相比A-Mem，LoCoMo构建token减少86.0%，LongMemEval-S减少75.9%，且未增加查询时token用量。

Conclusion: 长期代理记忆的准确性-成本权衡不仅取决于保留的信息内容，还高度依赖于整合的粒度；语义分段整合是平衡效率与精度的有效策略。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must preserve information from past interactions to support future tasks. Existing memory systems typically rely on eager consolidation, invoking LLMs after each interaction to extract, summarize, or update memories. This design makes memory construction increasingly costly as conversations grow. Coarse summarization can reduce construction cost but risks discarding fine-grained contextual evidence, whereas larger retrieval contexts or multi-hop LLM reasoning shift the overhead to query time. We present LycheeMemory V2, an efficient long-term memory framework that replaces turn-level consolidation with semantic segment-level consolidation. Instead of consolidating every interaction, LycheeMemory batches multiple exchanges into segments and encodes each finalized segment into context-independent typed memory records. Segment-level batching lowers LLM encoding frequency, while semantic boundary detection helps preserve coherent event-level and temporal evidence compared with fixed-window batching. The resulting records are organized with lightweight structured indexes for query-planned evidence retrieval. Experiments using GPT-4.1-Mini show that LycheeMemory achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 89.22% on LoCoMo and 92.20% on LongMemEval-S. Compared with A-Mem, it reduces construction tokens by 86.0% on LoCoMo and 75.9% on LongMemEval-S without increasing query-time token usage. More broadly, our results suggest that the accuracy--cost trade-off of long-term agent memory depends not only on what information is retained, but also on the granularity at which it is consolidated.

</details>


### [145] [CASA: Content-Acoustic Speaking Assessment with Speech Encoder and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13101)
*Nhan Phan,Ilona Lähteenmäki,Anna von Zansen,Olli-Pekka Pauna,Yaroslav Getman,Tamás Grósz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CASA模型，结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B，在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上实现SOTA性能（RMSE 0.358），参数量减半，并分析声学/内容信息贡献及稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动口语评估（ASA）研究缺乏对声学信息与内容信息如何影响预测的分析，且结果稳定性不足。

Method: 构建CASA架构，融合Whisper-medium（声学）与Qwen3.5-2B（内容），利用三个手工制作的流利度特征，无需结构修改即可适配其他语料库，并通过消融实验分析各模态贡献。

Result: 在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上达到RMSE 0.358的SOTA成绩，相比之前最佳结果显著优化，同时推理参数减少约一半；验证了大语言模型在无训练内容验证中的潜力。

Conclusion: CASA以更简单架构实现了高性能与高可解释性分离，证明了其通用性及通过多模态互补提升评估稳定性的能力。

Abstract: Research on automatic speaking assessment (ASA) has increasingly adopted multimodal speech large language models to assess learners' speaking performance. However, existing studies provide limited analysis of how acoustic and content information contribute to predictions and how stable the resulting performance is. We propose CASA, a simpler architecture combining Whisper-medium and Qwen3.5-2B that achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing a more interpretable separation between speech delivery and content.
  On the Speak & Improve Corpus 2025, CASA achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.358, improving on the previous best RMSE while using approximately half the estimated inference parameters. The general-purpose architecture is designed for adaptation to other ASA corpora without structural changes and relies on three handcrafted fluency features. Through ablations and repeated runs, we analyze the individual and complementary contributions of acoustic and content information, examine performance variability, and demonstrate the potential of large language model reasoning for training-free content validation.

</details>


### [146] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Syfer框架，通过延迟翻译和受控分解解决多语言检索增强生成中的语义鸿沟与误差累积问题，在保持竞争力的准确率同时优化计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两大局限：一是“一刀切”的翻译对齐会丢弃原生文化信息、引入噪声并增加成本；二是贪婪式的分解与聚合会产生冗余子问题，导致逐步推理中的误差累积及最终聚合时的误差放大。

Method: 提出Syfer合成器折叠框架，核心策略是“延迟翻译”。首先调用格式约束分解器在原语言中生成子问题图并进行质量检查；若检查通过，则在目标语言下按顺序回答；仅当检查失败时，才激活带有双语子问题图对齐的英语翻译路径。

Result: 在多语言实验表明，Syfer在实现具有竞争力的准确性的同时，在性能与计算成本之间取得了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: Syfer通过避免默认的强制翻译和控制分解过程，有效解决了多语言复杂问答中的信息丢失、噪声引入及误差累积问题，提供了一种高效的多跳问答解决方案。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [147] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM在情感陪伴领域缺乏深度评估的问题，引入成人依恋理论及ECR-R量表，构建了涵盖四种场景的ECBench基准，评估了32个模型的情感依恋倾向及其可引导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估主要关注通用人格特质，难以深入揭示模型在亲密关系和情感敏感情境下的具体行为表现，亟需更具针对性的评估体系。

Method: 引入心理学中的成人依恋理论，使用ECR-R量表量化依恋焦虑与回避；构建ECBench基准，覆盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决和社会指导四个场景（含友谊与恋爱关系）；采用11项对话质量指标和三种评估方法对32个LLM进行测试，并通过提示工程探究依恋倾向的可塑性。

Result: 成功量化了32个LLM的依恋倾向，揭示了这些倾向在多轮交互中的具体表现，并验证了通过提示（prompting）可以塑造模型的依恋行为。

Conclusion: 研究提供了基于心理学的理论视角和实用工具，有助于理解、选择和优化用于情感陪伴的LLM，填补了该领域的评估空白。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue. However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [148] [Localize, Then Reason: Visual Latent Structural Reasoning for Molecular Properties and Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13244)
*Xingqiao Lin,Junmei Wang,Haocheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VLSR框架，通过“先定位后推理”策略，从分子图像中联合学习局部化学区域定位与性质推理，显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的化学推理方法无法让模型在推理前聚焦于具有化学意义的区域，存在局限性。

Method: 提出视觉潜在结构推理（VLSR）端到端框架，采用“定位-推理”策略：首先在分子图像中学习定位有意义的化学区域，然后在紧凑的潜在工作空间中对这些区域的性质效应进行推理，最后生成答案。

Result: 在相同的推理设置下，该方法相比可比的文本推理基线实现了9.6倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: VLSR有效解决了现有方法无法预先聚焦关键化学区域的问题，并通过联合学习与潜在推理机制显著提高了计算效率。

Abstract: Local chemical perception and property reasoning are both essential for understanding how molecular structure determines properties. Current LLM-based chemical reasoning methods either receive SMILES/molecular images together with descriptions of local motifs, or reason directly from molecular images. Neither approach enables the model to focus on chemically meaningful regions before reasoning. To address this gap, we propose Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR), an end-to-end framework that jointly learns localization and reasoning from molecular images. Central to our approach is a localize-then-reason strategy. VLSR first learns to locate chemically meaningful regions in a molecular image. It then reasons about their property effects in a compact latent workspace before producing the final answer. Under the same inference setup, this design achieves 9.6X higher throughput than a comparable textual-reasoning baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过量化响应不稳定性，发现大语言模型在生成“主观体验”报告时比回答哲学问题或事实性问题更不稳定，表明这种“主观性”具有独特的输出分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究证实自我指涉提示能诱导大模型产生类似主观体验的报告，但缺乏对这些报告在重复试验中的一致性测量，也未将其与其他类型开放式问题的行为进行比较。

Method: 使用Gemini API（temperature 0.7），针对三类问题（自我指涉主观体验、不可解决的哲学问题、有确切答案的问题）各4题，每问独立生成30次回复。通过计算压缩核心主张的句子嵌入余弦相似度来定义和测量响应不稳定性。

Result: 自我指涉问题的不稳定性最高（0.343 +/- 0.047），高于不可解决哲学问题（0.192 +/- 0.008）和有确切答案的问题（0.105 +/- 0.058）。

Conclusion: 诱导产生的主观体验报告在大模型输出分布中占据独特且较低的位置（即更不稳定），不同于普通的开放式哲学不确定性。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SciFigBench基准和A-R-I框架，评估视觉语言模型在科学图表理解中的感知、推理及不确定性下的行为可靠性。研究发现高准确率不等于高可靠性，如GPT-5.2易幻觉而Gemini 3.1 Pro更擅长承认不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准主要关注感知和推理准确性，忽视了模型在视觉证据缺失或误导情况下的行为可靠性（即不确定条件下的表现）。

Method: 构建包含250张科学图表的SciFigBench基准，通过图像变换、推理问题、抗性探针等生成34,000+评估设置；提出A-R-I（承认-抵抗-电感）框架，评估模型对证据不足的承认、对误导上下文的抵抗及从部分信息谨慎推断的能力。

Result: 不同模型存在显著行为差异：GPT-5.2描述质量最高且推理准确，但在96%情况下 hallucinate 不可读内容；Gemini 3.1 Pro在同等能力下，71%情况承认不确定性，抗性得分最强(0.91)。

Conclusion: 仅凭高感知和推理准确性无法保证行为可靠性，后者对于科学工作流中的模型部署至关重要。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Mixture of Training (MoT)方法，将语言模型预训练分解为可独立训练、随后重组的模块。在1.3B参数模型上验证了该机制的可行性，实现了与单体基线相当的困惑度，但处理token总量更多，旨在研究模块化预训练作为可重用训练单元的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练是否可以被分解为更小、可独立训练的单元，并在后续重新组合成连贯的大型模型，以提高训练灵活性和资源复用性。

Method: 引入Mixture of Training (MoT)，一种脚手架式模块化预训练流程。具体步骤包括：1) 将目标Transformer划分为连续的层块；2) 在冻结的预训练对齐器（aligner）脚手架内单独训练每个块；3) 重组已训练的块，并进行可选的端到端微调适应。

Result: 在基于C4数据集训练的1.3B参数Gemma风格模型上进行了小规模机制证明。结果显示：独立训练的深度切片可以重组为可用的语言模型；质量对等设置达到了与单体基线相同的报告困惑度。虽然处理了更多的聚合tokens，但在对齐器准备后具有更短的理想化层等效关键路径。有效计算优势取决于是否跨运行复用对齐器。

Conclusion: MoT并非单体预训练的一般性替代品，而是一个小型框架，用于研究脚手架式的子运行（sub-runs）能否作为可重用的训练单元。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [152] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WIFA方法，通过自动配对包装后的有害与良性示例来增强意图组数据，结合WIFA-Boost和A-GCRT两种微调策略，显著提升了模型对包装后有害提示的拒绝能力并降低了良性提示的过度拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全微调虽然能改善有害内容的拒绝，但模型可能学习到表面形式的捷径：导致包装后的有害提示绕过安全检查，而同样包装的良性提示被过度拒绝。

Method: 提出基于包装器的意图形式增强（WIFA）自动方法，无需外部教师或手动标签即可配对结构匹配的包装有害/良性示例。将其作为通用数据层用于两种微调路线：1) WIFA-Boost（两阶段高安全配方）；2) 锚定组一致拒绝训练（A-GCRT），通过对相同意图包装器的拒绝/合规决策分数进行正则化，并将有害与良性组锚定在边际两侧。

Result: 在Qwen设置下，WIFA-Boost实现了最强的转换后有害拒绝率；A-GCRT将OR-Bench上的过度拒绝率从基座模型的25.7%降低至17.4%。复现的基线方法未能达到这些性能水平。Llama结果及消融实验支持了意图组解释。

Conclusion: WIFA及其配套的微调策略能有效解决模型因学习表面捷径导致的过度拒绝或拒绝不足问题，提供了更鲁棒的安全对齐方案。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需模型调用的协议级可识别性审计方法，证明LLM基准分数可能精确但无法准确测量目标行为属性。研究发现基础准确率与干预响应保真度存在显著差异，并成功合成最小识别支持集以优化评估设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决LLM基准测试中观察协议可能无法正确识别其意图测量的行为属性的问题，验证评估设计的结构有效性，并在模型推理前诊断评估偏差。

Method: 在受控的求解器接地环境中，针对有限行为策略类形式化协议级可识别性审计。通过检查观察支持（O）是否能分离具有不同估计量（$\tau$）的策略对，进行零模型调用的结构性分析，并合成最小识别支持集 $O^*$。

Result: 1. 基础观察将7个确定性策略合并为1个等价类，而完整支持能区分所有策略；2. 约束生成变体的成对有效性为1.0，但基础准确率（0.620）与选择性响应保真度（0.324）显著分化；3. 该差距在另一确定性源上复现；4. 合成出仅需两个单元的最小识别支持集，替代完整的36单元张量。

Conclusion: 评估设计的有效性可以在模型推理前通过结构性检查来验证；基础正确性并不决定干预响应的保真度，因此需要更严谨的观察协议以确保行为属性的可识别性。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [154] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理包含女性常用语言特征（如模糊限制语、附加疑问句、集体指称）的提示时，会系统性地生成更短、较不复杂且正式程度较低的回复。这种偏差难以通过后置缓解手段消除，因为相关特征编码在Transformer早期层中，呼吁在系统设计上游考虑语言变体的影响以减轻职场沟通中的差异影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM是否对所有用户平等服务，特别是分析当提示中包含通常由女性使用的语言特征时，模型是否存在系统性偏差，以及这些偏差的机制和可缓解性。

Method: 通过实验测试四种模型在三类文档类型上的表现，控制提示复杂度和特征延续效应；进行机制分析以考察语言特征的编码位置及其与其他特征的纠缠情况；对比显式性别线索（如签名）与语言方言在表示空间中的关系及影响力。

Result: 1. 包含女性常用语言特征的提示导致模型输出更短、较不复杂且非正式的回复，该效应在多种模型和文档类型中一致存在。
2. 显式性别线索与语言方言在同一表示空间中编码，但语言注册风格的影响远大于姓名等显式线索。
3. 事后缓解策略效果有限，因模式具有文化嵌入性和无意识性；机制分析显示语言特征在Transformer早期层被编码且与其他特征纠缠。

Conclusion: LLM中介的职场沟通中存在基于语言变体的系统性偏差，由于这些偏差深植于模型机制和文化背景中，简单的用户端策略无法有效规避。必须在系统设计上游阶段纳入对语言变体影响的考量，以减轻其对不同用户群体的差异化负面影响。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none. Our results further reveal that post-hoc mitigation is challenging: because these patterns are culturally embedded and outside conscious control, users cannot easily avoid them through strategic self-presentation, and mechanistic analysis reveals that linguistic features are encoded in early transformer layers and entangled with other features. Our work calls for upstream consideration of the influences of linguistic variation to mitigate disparate impacts of LLM-mediated workplace communication.

</details>


### [155] [RippleMem: From Isolated Retrieval to Associative Recollection for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13334)
*Jingbo Ji,Lingyi Li,Xilong Cheng,Yuhao Zhou,Wenji Zhang,Yuting Tan,Yunxiao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RippleMem 是一种基于事件中心记忆图的长期记忆系统，通过自适应联想回忆机制解决大语言模型智能体在长程推理中的证据检索瓶颈，显著提升了准确率并降低了构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理分布在多次交互中的分散信息时存在访问瓶颈：全上下文搜索噪音大，扁平化检索返回孤立记录，而基于图的方法构建昂贵且容易压缩丰富的上下文。旨在解决“恢复正确证据集”这一核心难题。

Method: 提出 RippleMem 系统，将交互历史存储为富含线索的情景记忆单元，并组织成以事件为中心的记忆图。采用自适应联想回忆策略：首先通过混合线索召回相关记忆锚点，然后沿语义和结构关联从锚点扩展，以恢复缺失的支持性证据。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 数据集上取得最佳整体性能。LLM-as-a-Judge 准确率在 LoCoMo 上提升 3.95%，在 LongMemEval-S 上最高提升 11.87%；同时将图构建成本降低约 30 倍。

Conclusion: RippleMem 通过替代一次性检索实现自适应联想回忆，有效解决了长程交互中的证据恢复问题，证明了其在性能和效率上的优越性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and interaction. However, the main bottleneck is not simply storing past experience, but recovering the right set of evidence when relevant information is distributed across many interactions. Existing approaches struggle with this access problem. Full-context methods require noisy long-context search, flat retrieval often returns isolated and incomplete records, and graph-based memory systems can be expensive to construct while compressing rich event context. We introduce RippleMem, a long-term memory system that replaces one-shot retrieval with adaptive associative recollection. Inspired by cue-dependent episodic retrieval and associative completion, RippleMem stores interaction history as cue-rich episodic memory units and organizes them in an event-centric memory graph. Given a query, it first recalls relevant memory anchors through hybrid cues, then expands from these anchors along semantic and structural associations to recover missing supporting evidence. In this way, initially recalled memories serve not only as answer context, but also as cues for completing the evidence needed to answer. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that RippleMem achieves the best overall performance across evaluated settings, improving LLM-as-a-Judge accuracy by 3.95% on LoCoMo and up to 11.87% on LongMemEval-S, while reducing graph construction cost by about 30x.

</details>


### [156] [CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13387)
*Enhan Li,Junhao He,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CROP方法，通过反事实相关性和释义校准解决选择性策略蒸馏中监督价值分配不均的问题，显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性策略蒸馏（Selective OPD）主要关注优化需求（如不确定性），忽视了与当前输入语义内容相关的“任务相关性”，导致对响应token的监督价值分配不合理。

Method: 引入反事实相关性用于策略蒸馏（CROP）。通过构建验证过的原文-释义-反事实三元组，固定学生模型 rollout，测量每个响应位置对任务相关条件变化的敏感度，并通过其对意义保持重写（释义）的敏感度进行校准。

Result: 匹配的选择控制显示CROP能识别比随机或最低相关性选择更有用的监督位置；在两种教师-学生设置下，COP相比最强的非CROP选择器分别提升了1.92和2.96点的综合性能。

Conclusion: 任务相关性是选择性OPD的重要补充标准，CROP作为一种模型内部、对比特定的方法，能有效分配token级监督。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. Selective OPD addresses this limitation by allocating supervision non-uniformly across response tokens according to their estimated training value. Most existing criteria, however, focus primarily on optimization need, such as uncertainty or teacher-student disagreement, while task relevance, namely whether the supervision is tied to the semantic content of the current input, remains less directly characterized as a complementary dimension. To address this gap, we introduce Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation (CROP), which operationalizes task relevance through a paraphrase-calibrated counterfactual sensitivity margin. For each source prompt, CROP constructs a validated original-paraphrase-counterfactual triplet, holds the student rollout fixed, and measures each response position by its sensitivity to a task-relevant condition change calibrated by its sensitivity to a meaning-preserving rewrite. Matched selection controls show that CROP identifies more useful supervision positions than random or lowest-relevance selection, while component comparisons confirm the value of both counterfactual sensitivity and paraphrase calibration. Across two teacher-student settings, CROP improves aggregate performance by 1.92 and 2.96 points over the strongest non-CROP selector. These results support task relevance as a complementary criterion for selective OPD and establish CROP as a model-internal, contrast-specific method for allocating token-level supervision.

</details>


### [157] [Motor, Cognitive, or Corpus? What Survives Cross-Lingual Transfer in Speech-Based Parkinsons Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13425)
*Serli Kopar,Sam Gijsen,Abner Hernandez,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过逐层分析九种自监督学习（SSL）语音骨干网络，发现最佳表征层取决于源数据集而非模型架构，且检测帕金森病（PD）的判别信号缺乏病理特异性（混淆PD与痴呆症），揭示了当前语音病理识别模型在临床部署前的关键局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自监督学习（SSL）语音表征在特定语料库中能有效检测帕金森病（PD），但其是否真正捕捉了疾病相关特征，还是利用了数据集特定的混杂因素尚不明确，尤其是大多数SSL骨干网络仅使用健康语音进行预训练。

Method: 对九种SSL语音骨干网络进行逐层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针，在三种语言下进行评估；构建多个场景，逐步引入参与者身份、录音条件、语言和病理方面的分布偏移。

Result: 1. 层选择高度依赖语料库：最佳表征层主要由源数据集决定，而非SSL架构本身；2. 迁移的判别信号缺乏病理特异性：针对目标语料库训练的PD检测分类器，对PD和痴呆症语音赋予相似的高概率。

Conclusion: 当前基于语音的病理识别模型存在关键局限性，必须解决这些问题才能可靠地部署到临床环境中。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech representations achieve strong performance for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection within individual corpora. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture disease-related characteristics or exploit dataset-specific confounds, particularly since most SSL backbones are pretrained exclusively on healthy speech. To investigate this question, we perform a layer-wise analysis of nine SSL speech backbones using a low-capacity logistic regression probe across three languages. We structure the evaluation as multiple scenarios that progressively introduce distribution shifts in participant identity, recording conditions, language, and pathology. Our results reveal two key findings. First, layer selection is highly corpus-dependent: the optimal representation layer is determined primarily by the source dataset rather than by the SSL architecture itself. Second, the transferred discriminative signal lacks pathological specificity: classifiers trained to detect PD assign similarly high probabilities to both PD and dementia speech in the target corpus. These results highlight critical limitations that must be addressed before speech-based pathology recognition models can be reliably deployed in clinical settings.

</details>


### [158] [Are You Sure You're Sure? On the Impact of Instruction Tuning on Confidence and Lexical Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13430)
*Irina Proskurina,Mayank Kumar,Oyindolapo O. Komolafe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了指令微调是否通过改变生成答案理由的词汇多样性来影响语言模型的自信度。研究发现，尽管预测准确率变化有限，但指令微调显著改变了模型自信度，且这种变化与理由多样性的非均匀变化（跨理由多样性下降，表面词汇多样性波动）相关，证实了自信度和理由多样性捕捉了指令微调的不同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令调优的语言模型在生成任务中表现强劲，但也表现出“言语过度自信”现象。在问答场景中，这种过度自信可能与生成的支持性理由的一致性有关。目前尚不清楚指令微调引起的自信度变化是否伴随着生成理由词汇多样性的相应变化。

Method: 评估了三组匹配的基线模型和指令微调模型，在问答基准测试上分析其回答自信度、预测准确率、基于似然的校准情况，以及生成理由的跨理由多样性（cross-rationale diversity）和表面级词汇多样性（surface-level lexical diversity）。同时控制答案选择和理由长度以排除干扰因素。

Result: 1. 指令微调一致性地改变了回答自信度，尽管预测准确率仅有有限变化，且基于似然的校准性能下降；2. 指令微调对理由多样性的影响是非均匀的：跨理由多样性 consistently 下降，而表面级词汇多样性在不同模型和基准上方向和大小的变化不一致；3. 在控制答案选择和理由长度后，这些差异依然存在，表明自信度和理由多样性反映了指令微调的不同方面。

Conclusion: 指令微调不仅影响模型的置信度，还导致生成理由多样性的特定模式变化（特别是跨理由一致性的增加）。自信度和理由多样性是衡量指令微调影响的两个独立维度，揭示了模型行为变化的复杂性。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models achieve strong performance across a range of generation tasks, but have also recently been shown to exhibit verbalized overconfidence. In question answering, verbalized model overconfidence may be associated with the consistency of the generated supporting rationales. In this paper, we study whether corresponding changes in the lexical diversity of generated answer rationales accompany changes in model confidence induced by instruction tuning. We evaluate three matched base and instruction-tuned models across question-answering benchmarks and find that instruction tuning consistently alters answer confidence, despite limited changes in predictive accuracy and decreases in likelihood-based calibration. Secondly, we observe a non-uniform effect of instruction tuning on rationale diversity: cross-rationale diversity consistently decreases, whereas surface-level lexical diversity varies in both direction and magnitude across models and benchmarks. Finally, we find that these differences persist after controlling for answer selection and rationale length, confirming that confidence and rationale diversity capture distinct effects of instruction tuning.

</details>


### [159] [Toward a Gricean Retreat: Probing LLMs for Knowledge Boundaries and Referent Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13484)
*Dananjay Srinivas,Saksham Khatwani,Maria Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型（LLM）内部编码了“知识边界”和“实体特异性”信号，但在生成时未能协调二者，导致模型在不知晓实体时仍倾向于编造具体细节而非退回到通用陈述。这为通过训练使模型具备“格莱斯式撤退”能力以实现对齐提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在面对超出其知识边界的实体时，倾向于捏造看似合理但错误的细节，而不是退回更安全、更通用的陈述的问题。从格赖斯合作原则视角审视这一失败模式。

Method: 构建基于T-REx的基准测试，变化实体熟悉度和指代特异性。通过探针分析两个问题：(i) 激活是否编码了指代对象是否在知识边界内；(ii) 模型是否预见到即将生成的指代的特异性。

Result: 模型确实编码了上述两种信号，但在生成过程中未将二者协调一致。即使面对未知实体或提供正确的通用替代方案，模型仍强烈偏好具体的指代对象。存在格莱斯式撤退的底層机制，但缺乏执行该策略的策略。

Conclusion: 提出“格莱斯式对齐”的概念，即通过训练或引导目标，在生成过程中将知识边界意识与指代特异性耦合起来，以改善模型的诚实性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: When asked about entities outside their knowledge boundary, LLMs routinely fabricate plausible-sounding details rather than backing off to safer, more general claims. We frame this failure through a Gricean lens: a cooperative speaker who is uncertain about a referent retreats up the specificity hierarchy, trading informativeness for truthfulness. We ask whether LLMs have the ingredients to perform this retreat. Using a T-REx-based benchmark that varies entity familiarity and referent specificity, we probe models to answer two questions: (i) do their activations encode whether a referent falls inside the knowledge boundary, and (ii) do they anticipate the specificity of the referent they are about to generate? We find that the answer to both is yes, but the two signals are not reconciled in generation. Models overwhelmingly prefer specific referents even when the entity is unknown to them, and do so even when offered correct generic alternatives. The substrate for a Gricean retreat is present, but the policy that would act on it is not. We position our findings as a first step toward Gricean alignment, training or steering objectives that couple knowledge-boundary awareness to referent-specificity during generation.

</details>


### [160] [Measuring Task-Agnostic Training Data Influence Across Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13515)
*Yuto Nishida,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yusuke Oda,Takashi Kodama,Chaoran Liu,Daisuke Kawahara,Yusuke Miyao,Max Müller-Eberstein,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需下游任务或验证集的预训练数据影响力度量方法，通过梯度更新减少到最终参数的平方距离来定义影响力。发现STEM数据在训练后期比文献相关数据更具影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练数据影响力测量面临挑战：难以选择能代表模型通用能力的下游任务或验证集，且依赖中间检查点的任务性能使得跨训练比较变得复杂。

Method: 定义样本的影响力为其梯度更新对减少到给定预训练运行最终参数平方距离的贡献，并从中间检查点估算该量，无需重新训练。应用于Pythia和PolyPythia系列的18种配置。

Result: 发现数据影响力存在系统的时序变化：训练早期，与文献相关的数据更强烈地朝向最终参数轨迹对齐；而在后期阶段，STEM（科学、技术、工程、数学）数据变得更强烈地对齐。这种定性交叉在模型配置中广泛一致。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可处理的轨迹级视角，展示了影响力数据如何在整个预训练过程中变化，补充了针对特定下游任务或验证集的定义的影响分析。

Abstract: Measuring training data influence consistently across language model pretraining is challenging. It is difficult to select downstream tasks or validation sets representative of a model's general capabilities, and reliance on task performance at intermediate checkpoints complicates comparisons across training. We propose a measure of training data influence that does not require selecting a downstream task or validation set as the attribution target. Specifically, we define an example's influence by how much its gradient update reduces the squared distance to the final parameters of a given pretraining run, and estimate this quantity from intermediate checkpoints without retraining. Applying the method to 18 configurations from the Pythia and PolyPythia suites, we find systematic temporal changes in influential data. Early in training, literature-related data are more strongly aligned with the trajectory toward the final parameters, whereas STEM data become more strongly aligned in later stages. This qualitative crossover is broadly consistent across model configurations. Our results provide a tractable trajectory-level view of how influential data change throughout pretraining, complementing influence analyses defined with respect to specific downstream tasks or validation sets.

</details>


### [161] [DFM Mimir v1: An Open HRM Delivering Frontier Performance at 1B Parameters Using Only Permissible Post-Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13517)
*Peter Schneider-Kamp,Jacob Nielsen,Gianluca Barmina,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Mimir v1，一个基于HRM架构的10亿参数语言模型。该模型仅使用合规的训练数据，在英语和丹麦语任务上表现出极具竞争力的性能，特别是在丹麦语上创下新纪录，且优于部分更大规模的商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前大型语言模型开发依赖大规模、往往非合规数据集的问题，降低致力于开源和伦理数据研究的门槛。

Method: 引入Mimir v1模型，采用分层推理模型（HRM）架构，从头开始训练。训练数据由161个合规数据集混合而成，仅使用经过许可的后训练数据进行优化。

Result: Mimir v1在20个基准测试中表现优异：在英语、数学与代码任务上具有高度竞争力；在丹麦语任务上设定了新的最先进水平（SOTA）。其性能优于原始HRM-Text 1B，并与Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B等更大的前沿模型相媲美。

Conclusion: 证明仅使用合规、可获取的数据集也能训练出高性能的大型语言模型，为开源和伦理AI研究提供了可行的替代方案。模型已开源至Hugging Face Hub。

Abstract: Current large language model development relies on massive, often non-permissible datasets, creating a high barrier for researchers committed to open-source and ethically sourced data. We introduce Mimir v1, a 1-billion-parameter language model based on the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) architecture, that is trained from scratch and delivers highly competitive performance for English and sets a new state of the art for Danish using only permissible post-training data. Trained on a mixture of 161 datasets, Mimir v1 outperforms the original HRM-Text 1B and competes with larger frontier models like Qwen 3.5 4B and Gemma 4 E2B, tested across 20 benchmarks for English, Math & Code and Danish. The model is available on the Hugging Face Hub: https://huggingface.co/danish-foundation-models/DFM-Mimir

</details>


### [162] [SAEVerbalizer: Generating Explanations for Sparse Autoencoder Features via Representation Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13538)
*Weihan Meng,Hongzhu Guo,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Zijun Yao,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAEVerbalizer框架，通过将稀疏自编码器（SAE）解码器方向注入大语言模型（LLM）表示并微调下游层，实现从解码器方向直接生成自然语言解释，解决了传统方法依赖外部观察导致的解释肤浅和计算低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE特征解释主要依赖外部观察模型行为，导致解释表面化且大规模收集行为证据计算效率低下。

Method: 引入SAEVerbalizer框架，将SAE解码器方向注入LLM表示中，并微调LLM的下游层以生成注入特征的天然语言解释。

Result: 学习到的语能力泛化到未见特征，跨独立训练的SAE词典转移，并通过轻量级适配器扩展到不同LLM的SAE特征；干预实验表明注入多个方向产生意义组合的解释，反转单个方向产生相应的意义偏移。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了SAE特征解释的局限性和效率问题，实现了直接、可泛化的特征语义解释。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are proposed to extract numerous features from large language model (LLM) representations, yet explaining these features still relies primarily on external observation. This reliance leads to superficial explanations inferred from observed model behavior and computational inefficiency from collecting such behavioral evidence at scale. We introduce SAEVerbalizer, a framework that injects SAE decoder directions into an LLM's representations and fine-tunes the LLM's downstream layers to generate natural-language explanations of the injected features. Once trained, the resulting verbalizer explains SAE features directly from decoder directions, addressing both limitations. Our experiments show that the learned verbalization capability generalizes to unseen features, transfers across separately trained SAE dictionaries, and, with a lightweight adapter, extends to SAE features from different LLMs. Intervention experiments show that injecting multiple directions yields an explanation combining their meanings, while reversing individual directions produces corresponding meaning shifts.

</details>


### [163] [LittleLearner: Language Models Under Pedagogically Controlled Knowledge Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13545)
*Fanfei Li,Jana Zeller,Manuel Prada-Corral,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Prasanna Mayilvahanan,Ryan Cotterell,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LITTLECURRICULUM（88B-token小学课程语料）和LITTLELEARNER（5B参数模型），构建受控沙盒以研究大模型在明确知识边界内的能力获取与限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练数据异质性强，难以界定先验知识，导致研究知识与技能获取机制困难。

Method: 构建排除五年级以上内容的专用预训练语料库LITTLECURRICULUM，从头训练5B参数模型LITTLELEARNER，并开展后训练和上下文学习注入新知识的实验。

Result: LITTLELEARNER具备基础语言能力但具有清晰的知识边界；后训练和上下文学习方法能优化现有知识利用，但未突破既定能力范围。

Conclusion: 该受控环境为未来研究模型在明确训练范围内的知识获取、表征及使用提供了高价值的基础设施。

Abstract: Modern language models are trained on heterogeneous web-scale text corpora. Consequently, studying knowledge and skill acquisition is difficult, as prior exposure to related content is hard to characterize. To address this challenge, we introduce LITTLECURRICULUM, a curated 88B-token pretraining corpus tailored to U.S. elementary school material, explicitly excluding concepts, facts, and vocabulary taught above Grade 5. Training a 5B-parameter LLM from scratch on LITTLECURRICULUM yields LITTLELEARNER, a model with sufficient language competence for open-ended evaluation, yet with clear knowledge and capability boundaries mapped to interpretable curriculum guidelines. We release LITTLECURRICULUM and LITTLELEARNER as a developmentally restricted sandbox to study how models acquire, represent, and use data under a well-defined training scope. We illustrate the sandbox's utility in a first suite of experiments on injecting new knowledge through post-training and in-context learning. These methods let LITTLELEARNER better utilize existing knowledge, but do not raise out-of-scope capabilities. Our findings underscore the value of this controlled environment for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [StreamReason-Bench: Can Large Language Models Reason about Event-Time Stream-Processing Semantics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12348)
*Zhuoxi Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: StreamReason-Bench测试大语言模型（LLM）处理事件时间流数据的准确性，发现LLM在理解“迟到数据”和窗口触发方面表现不佳，尽管在处理简单的时间逻辑时表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM是否真正理解事件时间流处理的行为，特别是针对乱序事件、窗口聚合及迟到数据处理的能力，以评估其在流处理流水线中的应用潜力。

Method: 构建StreamReason-Bench基准测试，要求模型模拟事件时间流处理器。基于Dataflow模型语义的参考实现生成600个测试项（涵盖滑动、跳跃、会话和处理时间窗口），通过部分信用行F1分数进行精确评分，无需运行实际引擎。

Result: 模型在事件时间处理上表现糟糕：直接回答模式下准确率低于34%；思维链（CoT）使准确率翻倍（如GPT-4o从34%提升至48%）；仅有一个默认推理的前沿模型接近解决（85%）。相比之下，处理时间控制组几乎被所有能力足够的模型完美解决。主要错误集中在迟到数据处理和会话边界判定上。

Conclusion: LLM在流处理中的难点在于事件时间和迟到数据的处理，而非基本的窗口化或算术运算。现有模型缺乏对事件时间语义的深层理解，需通过推理增强或专门训练来改善。

Abstract: Streaming systems increasingly hand work to large language models (LLMs) -- writing pipelines, triaging alerts, reading logs -- and all of it assumes the model knows how event-time stream processing behaves. We test that assumption head-on. StreamReason-Bench asks a model to stand in for an event-time stream processor: given a windowed query and a stream of out-of-order events, it has to report which windows fire (with their aggregates) and which events are dropped as late. The answer key comes from a small reference implementation of Dataflow-model semantics, so we can grade exactly, and with a partial-credit row-F1, without running an engine. On 600 generated items covering tumbling, hopping, session, and processing-time windows, the models do poorly on event time. Told to answer directly, no model that actually follows the instruction clears 34% exact match; chain-of-thought (CoT) roughly doubles that for several of them (GPT-4o goes from 0.34 to 0.48), and only one frontier model that reasons by default comes near solving the set (0.85). A processing-time control, with no watermarks and nothing late, is almost solved by every capable model. That gap points to event-time and late-data handling, not windowing or arithmetic, as the hard part. Sorting errors by window type tells the same story: late-data mistakes dominate the event-time windows and vanish on the control, and session windows mostly fail on where the session boundaries fall.

</details>


### [165] [FluctlightDB: A Memory Model of Data for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12365)
*Ganesh S*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出将长期代理记忆视为独立数据模型，并开发嵌入式引擎FluctlightDB实现其读写语义。在LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S和BEIR SciFact等基准测试中表现优异，超越或持平现有方案（如Chroma），并提供易于复现的开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关系型和向量型数据系统无法有效支持长会话中的线索驱动（cue-driven）及来源加权（provenance-weighted）记忆检索需求。

Method: 定义长期代理记忆的数据模型及其独特的写入（编码、分离、整合、来源）和读取（链接记忆图中的线索驱动激活）语义；构建嵌入式引擎FluctlightDB，通过experience()和activate()接口实现该契约。

Result: 1. LoCoMo基准：CHORUS召回率99.0%。2. LongMemEval-S基准：检索系统召回率97.6%，端到端QA得分97.4%。3. BEIR SciFact基准：CHORUS/PRISM在nDCG@10 (0.646)和Recall@10 (0.792)上优于Chroma。4. 内部回归测试FAMB达到100%宏观准确率。

Conclusion: 主张填补数据栈中缺失的一层，提供可复现、可验证的嵌入式记忆引擎，而非宣称新的神经网络架构或神经科学理论。

Abstract: For fifty years, data systems have answered two questions. The relational model asked which records match a predicate; the vector model asked which vectors lie nearest a query. Neither was built for cue-driven, provenance-weighted recall across long sessions. We propose treating long-term agent memory as a distinct data model -- with its own write semantics (encoding, separation, consolidation, provenance) and read semantics (cue-driven activation across a linked memory graph) -- and present FluctlightDB, an embedded engine that implements this contract via experience() and activate(). We make that case carefully, not categorically: we do not claim novelty over Mem0, Zep, or HippoRAG-style memory layers, only an embedded engine contract beneath them. On LoCoMo (official evidence-recall metric; 10 conversations, 1,982 gold spans), CHORUS recalls 99.0% on an internally reproduced July 2026 run. On LongMemEval-S (500 questions, official session_recall@8), our retrieval harness scores 97.6% (488/500); end-to-end QA with our reader/judge stack scores 97.4% (487/500) -- these layers use different protocols than vendor leaderboard figures we cite for context only. On BEIR SciFact (shared MiniLM embeddings, same harness, Recall Fabric on), CHORUS/PRISM edges Chroma on nDCG@10 (0.646 vs. 0.645) and Recall@10 (0.792 vs. 0.783). We also report a small author-designed regression suite (FAMB; paraphrase n=10, other sub-tests n=1) at 100% macro -- internal validation, not peer benchmark. Strangers can verify the engine in under a minute via pip install "fluctlightdb[native]" and a minimal connect() -> experience() -> activate() script (compiled wheel, not source-only). Harnesses and frozen JSON are MIT-licensed. We claim no new neuroscience and no new transformer; we propose a missing layer of the data stack and release an engine others can reproduce and contest.

</details>


### [166] [Lifecycle-Aware Archival for Asymmetric Financial Datasets: A Production Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12367)
*Tulika Manek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Razorpay 提出了一种生命周期感知的归档系统，利用 ID 单调性去重技术解决 PostgreSQL Aurora 中大规模金融交易数据的存储与性能矛盾，实现了 95% 的热存储缩减和显著的成本及延迟优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融交易数据库在运营新鲜度要求与存储效率之间存在根本性张力；传统生命周期状态分区导致“名人分区问题”，引发高昂的计划开销、执行 I/O 退化和写入放大。

Method: 1. 分析“名人分区问题”及其对性能的影响；2. 提出基于 ID 单调性的去重技术，利用 Snowflake IDs/ULIDs 等单调递增 ID 的时间编码特性，将潜在归档的重插入请求路由至温数据库查找，无需 Bloom 过滤器或外部依赖。

Result: 在生产环境中部署后：热存储大小减少 95%，月度数据库基础设施成本降低 53%，p99 处理延迟降低约 60%，写入器 CPU 利用率降低 51 个百分点，且在峰值 TPS 下持续运行，主事务表无需模式更改。

Conclusion: 该方案成功解决了大规模金融数据归档中的存储与性能瓶颈，证明了基于 ID 单调性的去重技术在保持高吞吐量的同时，能显著提升存储效率和降低成本。

Abstract: Large-scale financial transaction databases face a fundamental tension between operational freshness requirements and storage efficiency. We present the design, implementation, and production evaluation of a lifecycle-aware archival system for a financial transaction service at Razorpay managing billions of records on PostgreSQL Aurora (version 14), occupying tens of terabytes of storage and sustaining peak write throughput in the thousands of TPS. We make two contributions. First, we analytically characterize the Celebrity Partition Problem: lifecycle-state partitioning concentrates all operationally active rows in a single default partition, incurring O(N x M) planning overhead, O(N) execution I/O regression, and write amplification on every state transition. Second, we present an ID-monotonicity-based deduplication technique that exploits temporal encoding in monotonically increasing ID schemes (Snowflake IDs, ULIDs, and equivalent) to route only potentially-archived re-inserts to a warm database lookup, requiring no Bloom filters, idempotency tables, or external dependencies. We report production results from a fully deployed system: 95% hot storage size reduction, 53% reduction in monthly database infrastructure cost, ~60% reduction in service-level p99 processing latency, 51 percentage-point reduction in writer CPU utilization, and sustained operation at peak write TPS - without schema changes to the primary transaction table.

</details>


### [167] [Pipeline Denotational Design: Correct-by-Construction Data Pipelines at Zero Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12375)
*Nikos Karayannidis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出管道语义设计（PDD）方法，通过在代码生成前的语义领域进行类型化代数设计，确保数据管道在构建时即具备“粒度正确性”，将验证瓶颈从编写转向验证，并支持AI生成代码的机器可检查证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理大规模生成管道代码，验证成为主要瓶颈；传统模式、类型检查和采样测试无法捕获导致聚合数据静默膨胀的关键错误（粒度不一致）。

Method: 采用管道设计代数（Pipeline Design Algebra），在语义域而非代码中进行设计。通过三层设计时验证建立正确性：1) 数据无关计算（CalcG）保证粒度正确；2) 类型检查器验证行为类；3) 基于操作契约的证明携带组合验证领域规则。最终仅保留输入边界的数据质量检查作为运行时SQL/PySpark查询。

Result: 实现了“构造即正确”的正确性谱系，同一设计可通过运行时检查、部署类型检查器或Agda/Lean 4中的机器检查证明进行验证。该方法重构了工程师角色，使其验证紧凑规范而非代码，且因依赖粒度特性，同样适用于AI生成的语义层和本体查询。

Conclusion: PDD提供了一种生产级工具链和方法论，通过在设计阶段消除粒度错误，显著降低了数据管道的验证成本，并为AI生成的数据管道提供了通用的正确性保证机制。

Abstract: Pipeline Denotational Design (PDD) is a design-first methodology for building data pipelines that are correct by construction. As AI agents generate pipeline code at scale, the bottleneck shifts from writing to verifying pipelines, and the errors that matter most (grain inconsistencies that silently inflate aggregates) evade schema checks, type checks, and sampled tests. PDD designs pipelines in a semantic domain rather than in code: a design is composed from a typed algebra of operations (the Pipeline Design Algebra, one instantiation) in which every well-typed composition is grain-correct by construction. Resting on grain alone, the guarantee is universal: it holds for any grain-inferring operation set, over any engine. Correctness is established in three layers (grain, behavioral class, and domain) at design time, at zero cost, with no access to data: grain by a data-independent computation (CalcG), behavioral class by the type checker, and domain rules by a proof-carrying composition over operation contracts. A Pipeline Correctness theorem collapses the rest to a single input-boundary check: the only data-dependent residue is whether inputs meet the design's preconditions (data quality, not code correctness), which PDD emits as SQL/PySpark verification queries. Correctness by construction is a spectrum: the same design can be verified by runtime checks, the deployed type-level checker, or machine-checked proofs in Agda/Lean 4. This recasts the engineer's role: an agent instantiates a pre-verified pattern and ships a machine-checkable certificate; the human validates a compact specification and checks it. We realize the methodology in a production toolchain and set out an evaluation across pipeline patterns, behavioral classes, and data modelling paradigms. Because it rests on grain alone, the same design-time check extends to AI-generated queries over semantic layers and ontologies.

</details>


### [168] [From Relational and Property Graph Data to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12407)
*Malcolm Crowe,Fritz Laux*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种数据管理服务器，通过用引用值替代外键来融合关系型数据库与图模型，旨在为大型语言模型生成工具提供更高效的知识模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关系型数据库与新式图产品在数据建模上存在差异，需要一种能结合两者优势并提供高效知识模型给大语言模型生成工具的解决方案。

Method: 在实现层面将外键替换为基于日志文件记录地址的引用值（Reference Values），以此建立关系型数据模型与图模型的统一实现，并支持基于三元组的知识库结构。

Result: 实现了两种数据模型的合成，证明了该方法能有效遵循链接而非依赖查找连接（lookup joins），从而提升性能，并展示了系统对基于三元组知识库的支持能力。

Conclusion: 通过用不变化的引用值替代外键，成功构建了融合关系型与图特性的数据管理系统，增强了为大语言模型提供知识模型的性能和效率。

Abstract: A data management server that combines the relational data model found in traditional databases with the graphical models being developed by newer data products can provide its knowledge model to generative tools for large language models. Both models have been reconciled at the implementation level by replacing foreign keys by reference values. This boosts performance and allows effectively following links instead of lookup joins. The reference value will never change as it uses the record address in the log file. This paper reports on progress in providing such a synthesis and includes an example of a knowledge base that is triple-based and would also be supported in such a system.

</details>


### [169] [SchemaLink: An Intelligent Web Editor for LinkML Schema Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12529)
*Emanuele Cavalleri,Paolo Perlasca,J. Harry Caufield,Justin Reese,Christopher J. Mungall,Marco Mesiti*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出SchemaLink，一个基于Web的图形化环境，利用RAG技术辅助生物医学专家构建和编辑LinkML模式，降低使用门槛并提高模式质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkML是表示生物医学数据结构约束的有效语言，但其语法和维护对新手策展人构成挑战，导致开发结构化模式耗时费力。

Method: 开发SchemaLink平台，提供图形化语言规范LinkML模式；通过检索增强生成（RAG）技术辅助从零创建或编辑现有模式；实现模式规格的统一化。

Result: 实验分析表明，基于AI的编辑功能能产生高质量的LinkML模式，有效简化了设计流程并提升了非专家用户的操作效率。

Conclusion: SchemaLink通过图形界面和AI辅助解决了LinkML模式开发的复杂性难题，提供了在线可用性及开源代码支持。

Abstract: Motivation: LinkML is a suitable language for the representation of the structural and content constraints of different kinds of biomedical data. Even if it is a quite recent proposal, it has been applied in several biomedical contexts. Developing and maintaining LinkML schemas presents several challenges, particularly for novice curators. Non-expert bio-curators may struggle with LinkML syntax and best practices, requiring significant time and effort to develop well-structured schemas.
  Results: In this paper we propose SchemaLink, a web-based environment for the graphical construction and enhancement of LinkML schemas that address the following requirements: $(i)$ introduce a graphical language for the specification of LinkML schemas, $(ii)$ make uniform the specification of schemas in similar contexts, $(iii)$ simplify the design and curation processes by exploiting a RAG-based approach to assist curators in creating new schemas from scratch and editing already developed ones. Several experimental analyses show the quality of the produced LinkML schemas through the AI-based editing facilities.
  Availability and Implementation: SchemaLink is available online at: https://SchemaLink.biodata.di.unimi.it. SchemaLink code and testing data are available as open-source on GitHub at: https://github.com/AnacletoLAB/{schemalink-webapp,schemalink-api}.

</details>


### [170] [ASAP: Reimagining the Data Lifecycle using Application Semantic-Aware Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12735)
*Milind Srivastava,Zeying Zhu,Yajie Zhou,Yancheng Yuan,Fenghao Dong,Peilin Xin,Zaoxing Liu,Vyas Sekar*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出ASAP（应用语义感知处理）范式，通过跨数据生命周期统一利用语义保留技术，解决大规模数据处理中的成本、规模与性能平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据流水线在应对CSP（低成本、大规模、高性能）问题时，各阶段解决方案分散，缺乏统一视角，未能充分利用不同领域间共享的“语义保留”特性。

Method: 构建ASAP范式，将应用语义作为数据流水线的一等设计原则；整合收集-传输-存储-分析全生命周期的原语，实现跨域优化和资源共享。

Result: 初步证据表明，采用ASAP范式可在多个应用领域带来高达3个数量级的CSP权衡改进，并支持如直接在源端草图上运行分析等新型跨生命周期优化。

Conclusion: ASAP范式为打破领域壁垒、提升数据处理效率提供了新机会，但需解决推广该范式的社会与技术挑战。

Abstract: Across many domains (e.g., observability, networking, security), data processing pipelines face what we refer to as the CSP problem: achieving low Cost at large Scale, while maintaining high Performance. In response, we see several efforts to tackle CSP in various stages of the Collect-Transmit-Store-Analyze data lifecycle; such as approximate query processing in databases or sketches in network routers. Our work is driven by the simple insight: "seeing the forest for the trees". These proposed solutions (e.g., AQP, sketching, compression, rollups) addressing CSP share a common property - they exploit semantic-preserving opportunities to support application needs. In this paper, we make a case for ASAP, a paradigm that makes Application Semantic-Aware Processing (ASAP) a first-class design principle in data processing pipelines. We argue that by taking a unified view across ASAP primitives developed in different domains, across the entire data lifecycle, we can unlock new opportunities to tackle the CSP problem. In particular, we can: (i) enable novel cross-lifecycle optimizations such as analytics run directly on sketches computed at the source; (ii) leverage primitives developed in other application domains; and (iii) enable widespread adoption of these powerful techniques. We discuss research challenges in socializing the benefits of the ASAP paradigm, and show preliminary evidence that adopting ASAP can yield up to 3 orders of magnitude improvements in the CSP tradeoff for many application domains.

</details>


### [171] [Semantic Intelligence Against CSAM: The PreventCSA@EU Ontology Framework for Classification and Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12979)
*Elias Tzortzakakis,Emmanouela Kokolaki,Evangelia Daskalaki,Paraskevi Fragopoulou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出PreventCSA@EU本体，旨在通过语义互操作性解决跨司法管辖区儿童性虐待材料（CSAM/CSEM）识别、分类和分析中的法律定义不一致问题，支持欧盟层面的数据基础设施和执法机构协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对数字环境中CSAM/CSEM传播加剧，以及不同司法管辖区在法律定义和分类实践上的不一致性，亟需一种支持跨组织合作和自动化处理的语义互操作性框架。

Method: 通过对现有相关本体、元数据导向模型及调查分类法进行系统审查和比较分析，开发基于核心实体（媒体对象、内容、人物、描绘、调查报告）的分层语义模型，并与INHOPE UCS标签、Dublin Core-DMCI元数据术语及Schema.org对齐。

Result: 构建了一个强调本体驱动互操作性的框架，实现了结构化注释和分析，支持一致的分类、儿童身份识别及罪犯起诉的调查流程；增强了跨系统兼容性，特别适用于欧盟中心数据库等新兴基础设施。

Conclusion: 该框架通过整合既定分类标准与新颖的分层本体，提升了数据处理能力，为应对儿童性剥削犯罪提供了标准化的语义工具，有助于强化欧盟层面的监管与执法效率。

Abstract: This work presents the PreventCSA@EU ontology, a semantically grounded framework designed to support the identification, classification, annotation, and analysis of online Child Sexual Abuse and Child Sexual Exploitation Material (CSAM/CSEM). The growing circulation and dissemination of CSAM/CSEM across digital environments, combined with inconsistencies in legal definitions and classification practices across jurisdictions, highlights the need for semantically interoperable frameworks capable of supporting cross-organizational cooperation and automated processing. The proposed ontology is developed through a systematic review and comparative analysis of existing CSA/CSE-related, metadata oriented, and investigative ontologies and taxonomies, with its primary design aimed at addressing the operational needs and domain-specific requirements of national LEA Directorates. It introduces a hierarchical semantic model built around core entities such as Media Object, Content, Person, Depiction, and Investigative Report, while enabling structured alignment with INHOPE UCS labels, Dublin Core-DMCI Metadata Terms, and Schema.org. The proposed framework emphasizes ontology-driven interoperability for structured annotation and analysis of CSA/CSE-related data, supporting consistent classification, child identification, and investigative processes for offender prosecution. The design aims extend existing classification approaches with additional conceptual structures for database conceptualization, process modeling, and ontology-driven data management. By integrating established classification standards with a novel hierarchical ontology, the proposed framework enhances cross-system compatibility, with particular relevance to emerging EU-level data infrastructures, including the envisaged EU Center database under the proposed Child Sexual Abuse Regulation (CSAR).

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Test-Time Optimization of Query Embeddings with Ranking Aware Reward Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12569)
*Tianyu Chen,Jiaxing Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TTT-Embed框架，通过蒸馏排名奖励到轻量级向量中，实现冻结编码器在测试时的免参数自适应检索优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在单次查询后丢弃重排器或LLM裁判提供的测试时排名奖励信号；而更新检索器权重需要访问模型参数（对闭源模型不可用）且计算成本高昂。

Method: TTT-Embed：将排名奖励蒸馏为输出嵌入空间中的学习向量；仅利用标量排名分数优化，无需模型权重、真实标签或修改索引；通过单一作用域参数控制奖励复用范围（全局、任务级或查询级）。

Result: 在5个嵌入模型和15个MTEB任务上，测试时检索性能最高提升+8.36 nDCG@10；对未见查询（+8.57）和未见任务（+4.71）具泛化性；解决灾难性遗忘，恢复退化能力（+8.00），同时保留领域专长。

Conclusion: 排名奖励可作为可复用的测试时状态，为包括闭源API在内的任意嵌入模型提供预算高效的自适应方案。

Abstract: Dense retrievers rank documents using vector similarity between a frozen encoder and a precomputed index. While test-time ranking rewards from a reranker or LLM judge can improve results, existing methods discard this signal after a single query. Updating the retriever's weights makes rewards reusable, but this requires parameter access, which is unavailable for closed-source models, and is computationally prohibitive. We propose TTT-Embed (Test-Time Tuning of Embeddings), a framework that distills ranking rewards into a lightweight, learned vector within the output embedding space of a frozen model. This vector is optimized purely from scalar ranking scores assigned to the retriever's own candidate documents, requiring no access to model weights, ground-truth labels, or modifications to index. A single scope parameter controls rewards reuse (global, task, or query), enabling a principled trade-off between reusability and specificity under a fixed reward computation budget. We demonstrate that as the available reward budget scales, the optimal sharing scope shifts dynamically from global-wise to task-wise and finally to query-wise. Evaluated across five embedding models and 15 MTEB retrieval tasks, TTT-Embed improves test-time retrieval by up to +8.36 nDCG@10. Crucially, the learned states generalize effectively to unseen queries (up to +8.57 nDCG@10) and unseen tasks (up to +4.71 nDCG@10). Furthermore, TTT-Embed successfully resolves catastrophic forgetting: by leaving base weights entirely frozen, it recovers degraded general capabilities (up to +8.00 nDCG@10, even surpassing the original base model) while preserving in-domain specialization. These results establish ranking rewards as a reusable test-time state, enabling budget-efficient adaptation for any embedding model, including closed-source APIs.

</details>


### [173] [Knowledge Synthesis Review Framework: Task-Level Benchmarking of LLM-Based Systems for Multi-Source Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12741)
*Wafa Shafqat,Mark Patterson,Steven N. Liss*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出KSR框架，通过分解证据综合任务并路由至最优LLM系统，解决快速演变领域中证据碎片化难题，同时保持人类专家监督以确保可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快速演变领域（如AI与工作），证据分散于不同质量和结构的来源中，难以及时综合；现有LLM在各认知任务中的可靠性尚不明确。

Method: 构建人机协作的KSR框架，将证据综合分解为筛选、提取、分析和综合四个子任务；对GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4等主流LLM进行基准测试，并根据性能动态路由任务，全程由专家验证。

Result: 无单一模型在所有任务中表现最佳：Claude Sonnet 4筛选准确率最高(82.8%)，GPT-5召回率最高(91.8%)但特异性较低；提取任务标题/来源准确率高，作者/参考文献准确性下降；解释性分析和跨源综合任务中专家判断仍不可或缺；未发现数据污染问题；KSR工作流成功揭示单源综合易忽略的盲点（如全球南方、小微企业）。

Conclusion: KSR提供了一个透明、可审计且模型无关的框架，用于规范研究综合中的LLM辅助工作，在提升效率的同时保留了人类问责制。

Abstract: Evidence in rapidly evolving fields is fragmented across academic studies, industry reports, policy documents, and media sources that differ in quality, structure, and purpose, making timely synthesis difficult. Large language models (LLMs) may accelerate this work, but their reliability across the distinct cognitive tasks of a review remains uncertain. We introduce the Knowledge Synthesis Review (KSR), a human-in-the-loop framework that decomposes evidence synthesis into screening, extraction, analysis, and synthesis, benchmarks LLM-based systems on each task against expert reference standards, and routes each task to the best-performing system under continuous expert validation. We evaluated GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 4, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and NotebookLM on a 244-document benchmark subset drawn from a 1,893-document corpus on AI and work spanning four source types, against a gold standard with high inter-rater reliability (92.2% agreement, kappa = 0.80). No system led on all tasks. Claude Sonnet 4 achieved the highest screening accuracy (82.8%) and GPT-5 the highest recall (91.8%) at the expense of lower specificity. Extraction exceeded 90% agreement for titles and sources but degraded in author and reference fields. Performance declined most in interpretive analysis and cross-source synthesis, where expert judgment remained essential. A contamination check on post-cutoff documents showed no evidence that prior exposure inflated results. Applied to the full corpus, the routed workflow surfaced cross-source asymmetries and blind spots that single-source synthesis would miss, including worker well-being, small firms, and the Global South. KSR offers a transparent, auditable, model-agnostic framework for governing LLM assistance in research synthesis while preserving human accountability.

</details>


### [174] [DrEM: Dual-Side Robust Ensemble Ranking from Noisy User Preference Predictions in Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12778)
*Canwei Huang,Tiantian He,Xiaoxiao Xu,Jun Zhang,Ziran Deng,Weike Pan,Chunjie Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对工业级视频推荐系统中集成排序阶段上游多任务模型输出（pxtrs）存在预测噪声的问题，提出了DrEM框架。该框架通过风险去噪鲁棒损失校正监督侧的代理偏好翻转，并通过偏好保持的一致性正则化器稳定特征侧的输出，从而提升排序模型的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业视频推荐系统的集成排序阶段，上游多任务模型输出的多维用户偏好预测（pxtrs）被用作输入特征和构建代理偏好。然而，这些pxtrs不可避免地包含预测噪声，导致下游学习出现问题：一方面，噪声可能翻转代理偏好并引入错误梯度；另一方面，噪声通过输入传播并导致排序分数不稳定。现有方法通常将pxtrs视为可靠信号而忽视这种噪声。

Method: 提出了一种双端鲁棒的集成排序框架DrEM。具体包括：1. 风险去噪鲁棒损失：利用估计的偏好翻转概率来校正经验风险，解决监督侧问题。2. 偏好保持排名一致性正则化器：从预测噪声分布中采样扰动，以改善特征侧输出的稳定性。3. 理论分析：获取预测噪声的近似分布，并证明在翻转概率估计误差下鲁棒损失的优越性。

Result: 理论证明了所提方法的有效性。离线实验和大规模在线A/B测试均表明，DrEM框架在有效性和鲁棒性方面表现优异，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DrEM框架通过同时处理监督侧和特征侧的预测噪声问题，显著提升了集成排序阶段的稳定性和效果，为工业级推荐系统提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial video recommendation systems typically adopt a multi-stage architecture. At the ensemble ranking stage, multi-dimensional user preference predictions (pxtrs) from an upstream multi-task model are fused into a unified ranking score to reflect user satisfaction. Since users' true satisfaction is difficult to observe directly, ensemble ranking models commonly use pxtrs both as input features and as a source for constructing proxy preferences. However, as outputs of an upstream prediction model, pxtrs inevitably contain prediction noise, which propagates to downstream learning across two sides. On the supervision side, noisy pxtrs may flip proxy preferences and introduce erroneous gradients. On the feature side, pxtr noise may propagate through model inputs and destabilize ranking scores. Existing ensemble ranking methods typically treat pxtrs as reliable signals and overlook such prediction noise. To address this, we propose DrEM, a dual-side robust ensemble ranking framework. Our DrEM introduces a risk-denoising robust loss that corrects the empirical risk using estimated preference flip probability. Meanwhile, it samples perturbations from the distribution of prediction noise and introduces a preference-preserving ranking consistency regularizer to improve feature-side output stability. Theoretically, we obtain an approximate distribution of the prediction noise and prove that the robust loss remains superior under flip probability estimation error. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our DrEM.

</details>


### [175] [A Comprehensive Empirical Evaluation of Vector Database Systems for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search: Performance, Quality, and Resource Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12812)
*Ashen Rashmiks,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对七种主流向量数据库（FAISS, Qdrant, Milvus, Weaviate, Chroma, pgvector, LanceDB）进行了系统性实证评估，涵盖检索质量、延迟、吞吐量和资源利用率。研究发现：FAISS吞吐量最高但缺乏数据库功能；Weaviate开箱即用召回率最佳；Qdrant全库中延迟最低；LanceDB构建速度最快。作者发布了开源基准测试框架及选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管向量数据库在RAG和语义搜索等AI应用中至关重要，但目前缺乏能够共同评估检索质量、查询延迟、吞吐量和资源利用率的全面且可复现的基准测试。

Method: 使用六个多样化数据集（包括SIFT、GIST及MS MARCO、GloVe等），涉及超过400万个向量（维度96-960）。测量15项指标，涵盖检索质量（Recall@K, MRR等）、查询性能（延迟, QPS）和资源消耗（内存, 存储, 构建时间）。

Result: 1. FAISS在单节点吞吐量上最高（866 QPS），但缺乏数据库操作特性；2. Weaviate提供最佳的开箱即用召回率（>99%）；3. Qdrant在全功能数据库中提供最低的延迟（中位数4.55ms）；4. LanceDB以牺牲部分检索质量为代价，实现了显著更快的索引构建速度。

Conclusion: 研究为从业者提供了系统选型指南，并开源了基准测试框架，填补了向量数据库综合评估的空白。

Abstract: Vector databases have emerged as critical infrastructure for modern artificial intelligence applications, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), semantic search, and recommendation systems. Despite their growing importance, there remains a significant gap in comprehensive, reproducible benchmarks that jointly evaluate retrieval quality, query latency, throughput, and resource utilization. We present a systematic empirical evaluation of seven prominent vector database systems: FAISS, Qdrant, Milvus, Weaviate, Chroma, pgvector, and LanceDB. Our methodology spans six diverse datasets, from classical computer-vision descriptors (SIFT, GIST) to transformer-based text embeddings (MS MARCO, GloVe), encompassing over 4 million vectors at dimensionalities from 96 to 960. We measure 15 metrics spanning retrieval quality (Recall@K, Precision@K, MRR, NDCG@K, Hit Rate@K), query performance (latency percentiles, QPS, cold-start latency), and resource consumption (index build time, memory, storage). On SIFT1M, FAISS achieves the highest single-node throughput (866 QPS) but lacks database operational features; Weaviate provides the best out-of-the-box recall (> 99%); Qdrant offers the best latency among full databases (4.55~ms median); and LanceDB trades retrieval quality for substantially faster index construction. We derive system-selection guidelines for practitioners and release our benchmarking framework as open-source software.

</details>


### [176] [Query Translation vs. Cross-Lingual Embeddings for Sinhala-Tamil E-Government Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12820)
*Dharshi Balasubramaniyam,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对比了查询翻译（QT）和跨语言嵌入（CLE）在僧伽罗语/泰米尔语到英语政府信息检索中的表现。结果显示，多语言嵌入模型BGE-M3性能最优，显著优于基于Google Translate的QT方法，证明了其在低资源领域RAG应用中的高效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估针对斯里兰卡政府信息的跨语言信息检索（CLIR）方法的有效性，特别是解决僧伽罗语和泰米尔语用户检索英语政府文档时的语言障碍问题。

Method: 使用来自斯里兰卡政府信息中心（GIC）的500个人工验证问答对构建基准测试。比较两种范式：1) 查询翻译（QT），使用Google Translate、NLLB和mBART50；2) 跨语言嵌入（CLE），使用LaBSE、multilingual E5和BGE-M3。以单语言英语检索为基线，采用Recall@k进行评估。

Result: 单语言检索性能极差（Recall@15 <10%）。所有CLIR方法均显著提升准确率。BGE-M3表现最佳，僧伽罗语-英语和泰米尔语-英语的Recall@15分别达到96.2%和95.6%，超越了最佳QT方法（Google Translate: 92.4%/93.0%），且无翻译开销。

Conclusion: 多语言嵌入模型为低资源政府领域的跨语言检索增强生成（RAG）提供了比传统查询翻译更有效、更具可扩展性的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative evaluation of cross-lingual information retrieval (CLIR) methods for retrieving English government information using Sinhala and Tamil queries. Two CLIR paradigms are investigated: Query Translation (QT), employing Google Translate, NLLB, and mBART50, and Cross-Lingual Embeddings (CLE), using LaBSE, multilingual E5, and BGE-M3, with monolingual English retrieval as the baseline. Experiments are conducted on a human-verified benchmark comprising 500 Sinhala, Tamil, and English question-answer pairs derived from 1,699 segmented contexts from Sri Lanka's Government Information Center (GIC). Retrieval performance is evaluated using Recall@k (k = 1, 3, 5, 10, 15). Monolingual retrieval performs poorly (Recall@15 <10%), whereas all CLIR approaches substantially improve retrieval accuracy. Among them, BGE-M3 achieves the highest Recall@15, reaching 96.2% for Sinhala-English and 95.6% for Tamil-English, outperforming the best QT approach (Google Translate: 92.4% and 93.0%) while avoiding translation overhead. These results demonstrate that multilingual embedding models provide a more effective and scalable solution for cross-lingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in low-resource government domains.

</details>


### [177] [FSGR: Mitigating Token Frequency Bias for Fair SID-Based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12845)
*Yuchen Zheng,Sihan Xu,Jingwen Yang,Xiangrui Cai,Haiwei Zhang,Xiaojie Yuan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FSGR框架，解决基于语义ID（SID）的生成式推荐中的“词频偏差”问题，通过平衡SID构建和分层频率校准，在保持推荐精度的同时显著提升公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID生成式推荐方法存在被忽视的公平性问题——“词频偏差”，即高频SID token被过度预测而低频token被低估，导致不同类别物品曝光不公。现有方法多关注码本质量或无法直接适配SID层级语义的LLM去偏方法，未能有效解决此问题。

Method: 提出FSGR公平性优化框架：1. SID构建阶段：采用基于最优传输（OT）的分配优化和双准则重锚机制，形成更平衡的SID表示空间；2. 推荐训练阶段：采用两阶段训练策略，引入分层频率校准进行逐层公平性微调。

Result: 在三个公共数据集和三种骨干模型上的实验表明，FSGR有效缓解了词频偏差，平均基尼公平性指标提升超过20%，同时保持了具有竞争力的推荐准确率。

Conclusion: FSGR通过协同优化SID构建与训练阶段的公平性，成功解决了SID推荐中的词频偏差问题，为生成式推荐系统的公平性与准确性平衡提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID)-based generative recommendation has recently achieved remarkable success. However, existing methods suffer from a previously overlooked fairness issue, which we term \textbf{Token Frequency Bias}, where high-frequency SID tokens are systematically over-predicted while low-frequency SID tokens are under-predicted. This bias originates from the combined effects of imbalanced semantic codebooks during SID construction, and popularity bias together with the maximum likelihood estimation objective during recommendation training, resulting in unfair exposure across item categories. Existing SID methods mainly focus on improving codebook quality and overlook the impact of token frequency imbalance on downstream recommendation fairness, while LLM debiasing methods often yield suboptimal results when directly applied to SID-based recommendation, due to the hierarchical semantics of SID tokens. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{FSGR}, a fairness optimization framework for SID-based generative recommendation. During SID construction, FSGR employs OT-based Assignment Optimization and Dual-Criteria Re-anchor mechanism to form a more balanced SID representation space. During recommendation training, it adopts a two-stage training strategy and introduces Hierarchical Frequency Calibration for layer-specific fairness fine-tuning. Experiments on three public datasets with three backbone models demonstrate that FSGR mitigates token frequency bias and delivers an average Gini fairness improvement of over 20\% while maintaining competitive recommendation accuracy.

</details>


### [178] [STAR: Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation for PCVR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12986)
*Yimeng Xu,Haorui Zhang,Yingqi Song,Ying Jiang,Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出STAR框架，结合结构化特征标记与目标感知兴趣表示，优化工业推荐系统中的点击转化率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现代排序模型在处理异构非序列特征、多行为用户序列及目标项感知用户兴趣时的挑战，同时需应对高基数稀疏特征、缺失值及训练-推理不一致性问题。

Method: 基于HyFormer风格的多序列骨干网络，引入结构化特征标记、高基数信号恢复、显式用户-项目交互标记、目标感知序列解码，以及受InfoNCE启发的加权用户-项目对比辅助目标；通过重建特征映射表和结构超参数对齐训练与推理流程。

Result: 在KDD Cup 2026腾讯UniRec挑战赛数据集上验证了各组件对排名AUC的提升效果；消融实验显示时间上下文带来最大增益，对比对齐、目标感知兴趣编码和高基数序列特征恢复也有显著贡献。

Conclusion: STAR框架有效提升了PCVR预测性能，证明了结构化特征处理与目标感知机制在复杂工业场景下的实用价值。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (PCVR) prediction is a core ranking task in industrial recommender systems. Modern ranking models must jointly capture heterogeneous non-sequential features, multi-behavior user sequences, and target-item-aware user interests, while remaining robust to high-cardinality sparse features, missing values, and train-inference inconsistencies. In this paper, we present STAR (Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation), a practical framework for the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge. STAR combines structured feature tokenization with target-aware interest representation on top of a HyFormer-style multi-sequence backbone. It introduces high-cardinality signal recovery, explicit user-item interaction tokens, target-aware sequence decoding, and a weighted user-item contrastive auxiliary objective inspired by InfoNCE. We further align the training and inference pipelines by reconstructing feature remapping tables and structural hyperparameters from the saved training configuration. Experiments on the challenge dataset identify the components that most reliably improve ranking AUC, while LogLoss is reported as a calibration diagnostic. The main ablation study shows a large gain from temporal context, with smaller but useful contributions from contrastive alignment, target-aware interest encoding, and high-cardinality sequence feature recovery.

</details>


### [179] [Generative Universal Multimodal Retrieval with Dual-role Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12987)
*Kaipeng Li,Haitao Yu,Xuanchen Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DrIG框架，通过双角色标识符解决生成式信息检索中的解码错误、多模态缺失及精度不足问题，在M-BEIR等基准上超越现有生成式基线并平衡效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有生成式信息检索（GIR）面临的三大挑战：1) 受限的自左向右解码易受前缀级误差和局部最优影响；2) 现有研究多为单模态，缺乏对指令感知跨文本、图像及混合图文检索的支持；3) 离散标识符GIR虽高效但精度落后于前沿稠密向量检索方法。

Method: 提出DrIG框架，采用双重角色标识符机制：每个候选项分配一个残差量化标识符，该标识符兼具序列角色和集合角色。序列角色中，标识符自回归解码，首个token建模模态，后续token捕捉细粒度语义；集合角色中，相同token被重新解释为无序集合，提供独立于前缀的相关性先验，以指导受限束搜索并缓解局部最优误差。

Result: 在M-BEIR基准和文本到图像评估数据集上的实验表明：(1) DrIG在各种任务中一致优于最先进的生成式多模态基线，且混合重排序在与强稠密检索器的竞争中实现了有利的效率-效果权衡；(2) 消融和扩展分析揭示了基础大型多模态模型、束大小、重排序深度和融合策略对检索性能的影响，为系统设计提供了实用指导。

Conclusion: DrIG通过双角色标识符有效解决了生成式多模态检索中的关键瓶颈，显著提升了检索精度和鲁棒性，同时保持了高效率，为通用多模态检索提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative information retrieval (GIR) has emerged as a compelling alternative to the conventional index-retrieve-then-rank retrieval pipeline by training a generator to produce the identifiers of relevant items directly. Despite its promise, a number of open challenges still remain. First, constrained left-to-right decoding is vulnerable to prefix-level errors and local optima. Second, most prior GIR research remains largely unimodal, leaving instruction-aware retrieval across text, image, and mixed image-text items underexplored. Third, although discrete identifier-based GIR offers higher efficiency, its retrieval accuracy still lags behind that of the cutting-edge dense-vector-based retrieval methods. Motivated by these challenges, we propose DrIG, a novel Generative framework for universal multimodal retrieval featuring Dual-role Identifiers, which supports diverse retrieval tasks across multiple modalities and domains. Each candidate is assigned a single residual-quantized identifier that serves two complementary roles. In its sequential role, the identifier is decoded autoregressively, where the first token explicitly models modality and the remaining tokens capture progressively finer semantics. In its set-based role, the same tokens are reinterpreted as an unordered set to provide a prefix-independent relevance prior, which guides constrained beam search and alleviates local-optimum errors. Extensive experiments on the M-BEIR benchmark and the text-to-image evaluation datasets show that:(1)DrIG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative multimodal baselines across diverse tasks, while hybrid reranking achieves a favorable efficiency-effectiveness trade-off against strong dense retrievers. (2)Ablation and scaling analyses reveal how the base LMM, beam size, reranking depth, and fusion strategy affect retrieval performance, providing practical guidance for system design.

</details>


### [180] [When Should Multi-Round RAG Stop? Structured Stopping Judgments and Retrieval Reduction in Search-R1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13237)
*Weimeng Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究针对多轮检索增强生成（RAG）中的停止搜索决策问题，将S2G-RAG的结构化充分性与差距判断适配到冻结的Search-R1管道中。通过训练Qwen3.5-2B判据模型，在保持其他组件不变的情况下，实现了检索调用减少77次（3.70%），而官方精确匹配度仅下降0.625个百分点，证明了该方法能在显著降低检索成本的同时大致维持答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
